- **ubersimplify**: Whole-codebase 3-lens simplification (Reuse/Quality/Efficiency). Packs the repo into a fixed fleet of byte-balanced areas (default 8), audits each area with ONE multi-lens code-simplifier, applies preserve-behavior fixes via code-fixer as one refactor: commit per area on a new branch, opens ONE PR for review, and files leftover blocker findings as GitHub issues. Whole-repo by default; pass a path/glob to narrow. --audit-only for a read-only scan. - **ui2-component**: Spec ONE UI 2.0 component from a Figma component/set link — ingest every designed variant and state, resolve it against the program registry (mint a new C-### row or enrich an existing one), write the component's normative contract under docs/ui2/design-system/components/, and update the registry + tokens. The component-level sibling of /ui2-screen for when the user pastes a component link instead of a whole screen. Spec-writing only, no app code. Designed to run in a FRESH session. ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-component.md))
- **ui2-component-build**: Build ONE specced UI 2.0 component as a capturable SwiftUI preview — resolve its dependencies, regenerate the tokens, write the view under iphone/MakeReadyUI/, generate its fixture + ViewRegistry case, capture it in the simulator, and diff the render against its frozen Figma snapshot. The build-side sibling of /ui2-component; these views ship — never hand-wired; the app wires them from `iphone/MakeReady/UI2/` containers (ui2-shell D2/D29). Designed to run in a FRESH session. ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-component-build.md))
- **ui2-component-delete**: Retire a UI 2.0 registry row that should never have existed or is no longer designed — `/ui2-component-delete &lt;C-###&gt; [reason]`. Strikes the row WITHOUT reusing its ID, removes its build artifacts, and reports every consumer it had. Refuses while any screen or component still renders it, because delete has no keeper to absorb anything. (Copied by the /components 2.0 UI's Prompt ▸ Delete dialog.) ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-component-delete.md))
- **ui2-component-merge**: Merge a duplicate UI 2.0 registry row into the row that survives — `/ui2-component-merge &lt;keep&gt; &lt;duplicate&gt;`. Absorbs the duplicate's designed states into the keeper's contract, repoints every consumer, screen spec, element map and build artifact, then strikes the duplicate's row WITHOUT reusing its ID. Destructive by nature: it proves the two are duplicates before it moves anything, and never deletes history. (Copied by the /components 2.0 UI's Prompt ▸ Merge dialog.) ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-component-merge.md))
- **ui2-component-update**: Verify ONE built UI 2.0 component preview against its spec AND its notes, fix what fails, recapture, and report. Derives the effective requirement set (contract + notes, newer wins per property), checks the built view and fixture against it, edits only MakeReadyUI/ and the fixture, and reports every place a note has overtaken the spec as drift for the owner to resolve. Never writes under docs/ui2. Designed to run in a FRESH session. ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-component-update.md))
- **ui2-resolve**: Resolve unresolved comments on a UI 2.0 component preview — one state (`C-034 default`) or every state (`C-034`). Figma is the reference; the SwiftUI preview view, its fixture, or — when the comment is an owner ruling — its contract is what changes. Edits, recaptures, verifies against the frozen snapshot, then replies + resolves. (Copied by the /components 2.0 UI's Prompt menu.) ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-resolve.md))
- **ui2-screen**: Spec ONE UI 2.0 screen — ingest its Figma design (or doc source), decompose it against the program component registry, write the normative screen spec, and update the shared program artifacts (registry, tokens, screen map, README manifest). Spec-writing only, no app code. With no argument, takes the next item from docs/ui2/README.md's dependency-ordered spec queue. Designed to run in a FRESH session. ([lukekeith/makeready](https://github.com/lukekeith/makeready/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ui2-screen.md))

The app keeps i ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0002-attach/commands/uitool.attach.md))
- **uitool.classes**: Exactly one of `--match` / `--class` is required — a bare `classes` would dump every loaded class, the unbounded read the surface forbids. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0010-classes/commands/uitool.classes.md))
- **uitool.detach**: 1. Resolve `&lt;app&gt;` to a target. 2. If the target is attached in the current session, instruct the injected server to close and unlink the socket and drop the registry ([[domain.uitool.ipc]] threading: on unload, close socket, unlink path, drop the registry; [[domain.uitool.injection]] lifecycle: detach → close socket, drop registry). 3. If the target is not attached (or already detached), treat th ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0002-attach/commands/uitool.detach.md))
- **uitool.doctor**: There is no `--json` flag: JSON is the only output. The consumer is a coding agent, not a human at a TTY, so output is always deterministic JSON on stdout ([[architecture]]) — there is no human-readable mode to toggle. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0001-doctor/commands/uitool.doctor.md))
- **uitool.find**: At least one of `--class` / `--where` is required. `find &lt;app&gt;` with neither is a **usage error** (exit 2, see [[error.uitool.find-bad-selector]]): an unconstrained full enumeration is exactly the expensive tree-download the cost-tiering exists to discourage, so the surface refuses it rather than silently matching every node. To enumerate broadly on purpose, pass an explicit broad selector (e.g. ` ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0005-find/commands/uitool.find.md))
- **uitool.inspect**: 1. Resolve `&lt;app&gt;` to its live session over the socket ([[domain.uitool.ipc]]); no session is `NOT_ATTACHED` (exit 4). 2. Send the `inspect` op with the node id, the `--invoke` flag, and the `--match` pattern. 3. The server resolves the node id to its live object through the [[domain.uitool.registry]] — re-walking the structural path and passing the four-gate validation (epoch, path, pointer valid ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0007-inspect/commands/uitool.inspect.md))
- **uitool.launch**: `launch` brings a target up **fresh** with the inspector already loaded — the cooperative **launch path** ([[domain.uitool.injection]]): `posix_spawn` under `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES=…/UIToolBoot.dylib` so the [[domain.uitool.boot]] dylib loads before `main` and starts the [[domain.uitool.server]]. Use it when a clean launch state is wanted. To inspect an app **as it sits right now**, preserving its ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0002-attach/commands/uitool.launch.md))
- **uitool.list-apps**: `list-apps` is **pure local detection** — like [[command.uitool.doctor]] it runs before any injection and does **not** open the IPC socket or contact a target ([[domain.uitool.ipc]] is the post-injection protocol, unused here). It enumerates the running processes that are candidates for attachment and annotates each: ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0001-doctor/commands/uitool.list-apps.md))
- **uitool.node**: The drill step of the canonical loop (locate few → project narrow → **read deep on the survivors**). Reads a single node the agent has already located and pulls the on-demand facets the default tree/find projection omits. It does not walk a subtree and does not search. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0006-node/commands/uitool.node.md))
- **uitool.schema**: No target, no `--snapshot`, no injection — `schema` is a pure static read of the contract the other verbs emit. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0008-schema/commands/uitool.schema.md))
- **uitool.signing**: No injection, no attach — `signing` reads the signature off the target's binary. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0009-signing/commands/uitool.signing.md))
- **uitool.tree**: The structural-exploration verb. Walks the view hierarchy from a chosen root down a finite depth, projects a chosen set of fields per node, and emits an explicit truncated marker at every branch cut off by the depth limit. It never walks the whole tree — `--depth` is always finite and small by default. The coding agent is the consumer; output is machine-first. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0004-tree/commands/uitool.tree.md))
- **uitool.windows**: 1. Resolve `&lt;app&gt;` to the attached session's socket per [[domain.uitool.ipc]]; if no session is attached, fail per [[domain.uitool.ipc]]. 2. Issue the [[domain.uitool.ipc]] `hierarchy` op with `maxDepth: 0` and `window: "all"` to the injected server. `maxDepth: 0` projects window roots only — no descendant walk — which is exactly what this verb returns; there is no dedicated `windows` op. The serv ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0003-windows/commands/uitool.windows.md))
